Now, LBFGS softmax details:
```
def _softmax(x):
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e=np.exp(x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```
For gradient, if `a` has zeros (due to underflow), grad_x zeros for those; okay.
`_lse_logit_value_grad(x,beta)`:
   if time? no.
   a=_softmax(x)
   val, grad_a = _lse_fixed(a,beta)
   corr=np.dot(a,grad_a)
   g=a*(grad_a-corr)
   # optionally center again
   g -= np.dot(a,g) # should be zero
   return val,g
```
LSE fixed can take `n`? We'll pass as global? No, functions top-level cannot have closures but can accept args.

We should avoid repeated allocation of zeros large? fine.

import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m): ...

def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    M=2*n-1; N=_next_pow2(M); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]; return np.maximum(out,0)

def _exact_score(a):
 a=np.asarray... n; S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12: return inf
 c = _fast_self_conv(a)
 if c.size==0: return inf
 # if due roundoff max negative? max=0
 mx=float(c.max())
 if mx<=0: return inf? if S>0, at least some positive; use max(mx,0)
 return float(2.0*n*mx/(S*S))

`_normalize(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(float); a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0); a=np.clip(a,0,None); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-15: return np.ones(n)/n; return a/S
```

`_resample(a,n)`:
```
a=normalize; m=size; if m==n return copy; if m<2 return ones; old=np.linspace(0,1,m); new=np.linspace(0,1,n); return np.interp(new,old,a)
```

`_softmax(x)`:

x=np.asarray(x,float); x=np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0, posinf=0, neginf=0)
xmax=x.max(); z=x-xmax; e=np.exp(np.clip(z,-700,700)); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return uniform; return e/s
```

`_conv_corr_sparse(a, idx, weights)`:
```
n=len(a); R=np.zeros(n);
for k,wt in zip(idx,weights):
 if wt==0: continue
 k=int(k)
 i_start=max(0,k-(n-1)); i_end=min(n-1,k)
 if i_start>i_end: continue
 j_start=k-i_end; j_end=k-i_start
 R[i_start:i_end+1] += wt * a[j_start:j_end+1]
return R
```
Check a index: if i=0,k=1,n=2: i_start=0,i_end=1, j_start=0,j_end=1, R[0:2]+=a[0:2] gives R0=a0? R_i=a_{i? want a_{1-i}: R0=a1, R1=a0. Our slice j_start=k-i_end=1-1=0, j_end=k-i_start=1; a[0:2]=[a0,a1] assigned to i_start:2 [0,1] => R0=a0 wrong! Need orientation. We need R[i]=a[k-i]. As i increases from i_start to i_end, j decreases from k-i_start to k-i_end. So source slice reversed.
```

j_end = k - i_end

```

`_lse_fixed(a,beta)`:
```
a=normalize(a); n=a.size; c=_fast_self_conv(a); cmax=float(c.max())
if cmax<=0 or not finite: return 0, zeros
d=n*c
dmax=n*cmax
z=beta*(d-dmax); z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
e=np.exp(z); se=e.sum(); if se<=0 or inf: fallback top? 
w=e/se
L=dmax + np.log(se)/beta
val=2*L
R=_conv_corr_fft(a,w)
grad=4.0*n*R # correct maybe
grad=np.nan_to_num(grad)
return float(val), grad
```

`_lse_logit_value_grad(x,beta)`:
```
a=_softmax(x)
val,grad_a=_lse_fixed(a,beta)
corr=float(np.dot(a,grad_a))
grad_x=a*(grad_a-corr)
# re-center to remove drift
corr2=float(np.dot(a,grad_x))
if abs(corr2)>1e-12: grad_x-=corr2
grad_x=np.nan_to_num(grad_x)
return val, grad_x
```
Potential beta not passed? top-level.

`_lbfgs_lse(a0,beta,deadline,label="")`:
```
a=normalize(a0); n=len(a)
# ensure positive for log
a_pos=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
y=np.log(a_pos)
# check time before
if time.monotonic() > deadline-5: return exact(a), a
# measure first
tf=time.monotonic(); val,grad=_lse_logit_value_grad(y,beta); dt=time.monotonic()-tf
dt=max(dt*1.4,0.01)
rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
maxfun=int(max(20, min(600, 0.8*rem/dt)))
# If label print initially? no
opts={'maxiter':int(max(20,maxfun//3)),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-7,'maxls':20,'maxcor':10}
try:
 res=minimize(_lse_logit_value_grad, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,), options=opts)
 x=res.x
except Exception:
 x=y
a_out=_softmax(x); a_out=normalize(a_out)
sc=exact(a_out)
if not finite or sc>1e6: return exact(a), a
return sc,a_out
```
But `_lse_logit_value_grad` as callable with args? Scipy passes only x to function and ignores args? Actually functools? `minimize(fun, x0, args=(beta,))` fun must accept *args; our function signature `fun(x,beta)` will be called as `fun(x, beta)`. Good.
No closures.

After lbfgs, maybe if time exceeded due to measurement? maxfun bound. We cannot check deadline inside minimize. Assume okay.

`_mirror_pass(a0,deadline,max_iters=None,k_active=3,tol=1e-6,eta_start=2.0,label="")`:

```python
"""
Search for non-negative coefficient sequences minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The search combines:
1. mathematically motivated one-sided power-law / square-root initializers,
2. low-dimensional deterministic families and parent resampling,
3. L-BFGS-B on a log-softmax (mirror) parameterization of an LSE-smoothed
   peak-convolution objective,
4. exact active-set mirror descent for final non-smooth polishing,
5. controlled random restarts and a final intensive polish.

All sequences are kept non-negative and normalized to sum 1.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (S * S))


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / S


def _resample(a, n):
    a = _normalize(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xmax = float(x.max())
    z = x - xmax
    e = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / x.size
    return e / s


def _conv_corr_sparse(a, idx, weights):
    """
    R_i = sum_k weights[k] * a[k-i], with a[j]=0 outside [0,n-1].
    Sparse implementation for a small number of active convolution lags.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    R = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k, wt in zip(idx, weights):
        if wt == 0.0:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        i_start = max(0, k - (n - 1))
        i_end = min(n - 1, k)
        if i_start > i_end:
            continue
        j_start = k - i_start
        j_end = k - i_end
        R[i_start:i_end + 1] += float(wt) * a[j_end:j_start + 1][::-1]
    return R


def _conv_corr_fft(a, w):
    """
    R_i = sum_k w[k] * a[k-i], with dense w of length 2n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or w.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n)
    ar = a[::-1].copy()
    AR = np.fft.rfft(ar, N)
    WR = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(AR * WR, N)
    R = out[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(R, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0)


def _lse_fixed(a, beta):
    """
    LSE approximation to 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) for arrays with sum(a)=1.
    Returns (value, gradient wrt a).
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    cmax = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-18:
        return 1e12, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    d = n * c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = beta * (d - dmax)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    sum_e = float(e.sum())
    if sum_e <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sum_e):
        # Fallback to a very sharp active-set approximation.
        idx = int(np.argmax(d))
        w = np.zeros(d.size, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        L = dmax
    else:
        w = e / sum_e
        L = dmax + math.log(sum_e) / beta

    val = float(2.0 * L)
    R = _conv_corr_fft(a, w)
    grad = 4.0 * n * R
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return val, grad


def _lse_logit_value_grad(x, beta):
    """
    Objective and gradient for softmax parameterization a = softmax(x).
    This keeps sum(a)=1 exactly and avoids simplex projection.
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = _softmax(x)
    val, grad_a = _lse_fixed(a, beta)
    corr = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_x = a * (grad_a - corr)
    corr2 = float(np.dot(a, grad_x))
    if abs(corr2) > 1e-14:
        grad_x = grad_x - corr2
    grad_x = np.nan_to_num(grad_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return float(val), grad_x


def _lbfgs_lse(a0, beta, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return _exact_score(a), a

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now >= deadline - 3.0:
        return _exact_score(a), a

    a_pos = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    y = np.log(a_pos)
    y = y - float(y.mean())

    t_meas = time.monotonic()
    try:
        val0, grad0 = _lse_logit_value_grad(y, beta)
        dt = max(time.monotonic() - t_meas, 1e-3)
    except Exception:
        return _exact_score(a), a

    if not np.isfinite(val0):
        return _exact_score(a), a

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem < 5.0:
        return _exact_score(a), a

    # Be conservative: a single L-BFGS-B call should not overrun the deadline.
    maxfun = int(max(24, min(700, 0.75 * rem / max(dt, 0.01))))
    opts = {
        "ftol": 1e-14,
        "gtol": 1e-7,
        "maxls": 20,
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "maxiter": int(max(25, maxfun // 3)),
        "maxcor": 12,
    }

    out_a = a
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lse_logit_value_grad,
            y,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            args=(beta,),
            options=opts,
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            cand = _softmax(res.x)
            cand = _normalize(cand)
            sc = _exact_score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                out_a = cand
    except Exception:
        out_a = a

    sc = _exact_score(out_a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        out_a = a
        sc = _exact_score(a)
    return float(sc), out_a


def _single_active_grad(a, k_active, top_only=True):
    """
    Exact subgradient for the active max coefficient(s).
    Returns c, score, gradient (wrt a times score factor).
    """
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    c = _fast_self_conv(a)
    d = n * c
    dmax = float(d.max())
    score = float(2.0 * dmax)

    if not np.isfinite(dmax):
        return c, score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    k = int(k_active)
    if k <= 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(d))], dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        k = min(k, d.size)
        idx = np.argpartition(d, -k)[-k:]
        # Optionally keep only top values; ties are arbitrary but acceptable.
    m = idx.size
    wts = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    R = _conv_corr_sparse(a, idx, wts)
    grad = 4.0 * n * R
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, score, grad


def _mirror_pass(a0, deadline, k_active=3, eta_start=2.0, max_iters=None):
    """
    Mirror-descent style subgradient polishing on the simplex / softmax.
    Uses exact active-set gradients and normalized multiplicative updates.
    """
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _exact_score(a)

    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - now))
    if max_iters is None:
        max_iters = int(max(80, min(25000, rem * 18.0)))
    max_iters = int(max(1, max_iters))

    eta = float(eta_start)
    no_improve = 0
    n = a.size

    for it in range(max_iters):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline - 1.0:
            break

        _, score, grad = _single_active_grad(a, k_active)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(eta * 1.08, 12.0)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve >= 40:
                eta *= 0.82
                no_improve = 0
                if eta < 0.03:
                    break

        corr = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        g = grad - corr * a
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn <= 1e-15:
            break

        step = -eta * (g / gn)
        step = np.clip(step, -1.2, 1.2)
        a2 = a * np.exp(step)
        a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, None)
        S = float(a2.sum())
        if S <= 1e-15:
            a2 = a.copy()
        else:
            a2 /= S

        sc2 = _exact_score(a2)
        if not np.isfinite(sc2):
            a2 = a.copy()
            sc2 = score

        # Accept mild deteriorations sometimes, but reject large ones.
        if sc2 <= best_score + 0.02:
            a = a2
        else:
            a = best_a

    if _exact_score(a) < best_score:
        best_score = _exact_score(a)
        best_a = a.copy()
    return float(best_score), best_a


def _root_sequence(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), i.e. central binomial / 4^m.
    A natural motif for flatter convolution peaks.
    """
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    h[0] = 1.0
    prev = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        prev *= (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
        h[i] = prev
    return _normalize(h)


def _add_init(inits, arr, cap):
    if len(inits) >= cap:
        return
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    S = float(arr.sum())
    if S <= 1e-15:
        return
    arr = arr / S
    inits.append(arr)


def _generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None):
    inits = []
    cap = 150

    # Basic candidates.
    _add_init(inits, np.ones(n), cap)
    root = _root_sequence(n)
    _add_init(inits, root, cap)
    _add_init(inits, root[::-1], cap)
    _add_init(inits, root + 0.15 * root[::-1], cap)
    _add_init(inits, root + 0.30 * root[::-1], cap)

    # Parent-based, if available.
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 32:
        p = _resample(parent, n)
        _add_init(inits, p, cap)
        _add_init(inits, p[::-1], cap)
        _add_init(inits, 0.65 * p + 0.35 * root, cap)
        _add_init(inits, 0.40 * p + 0.60 * root, cap)
        _add_init(inits, 0.25 * p + 0.75 * root, cap)

    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    y = 1.0 - x + 1e-12

    # One-sided power law families: x^-alpha * (1-x)^beta.
    alphas = [0.0, 0.08, 0.18, 0.30, 0.38, 0.44, 0.47, 0.49]
    betas = [-0.30, -0.15, 0.0, 0.10, 0.25, 0.45, 0.80]
    for alpha in alphas:
        if alpha == 0.0:
            base = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            base = np.power(x, -alpha)
        for beta in betas:
            if beta == 0.0:
                w = base
            else:
                w = base * np.power(y, beta)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(w)) and float(w.sum()) > 0:
                _add_init(inits, w, cap)
                _add_init(inits, w[::-1], cap)

    # Modulated power laws.
    for alpha in [0.35, 0.42, 0.47, 0.49]:
        base = np.power(x, -alpha)
        for amp in [0.25, 0.45, 0.70]:
            for f in [1, 2, 3]:
                w = base * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
                _add_init(inits, w, cap)
                w = base * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x))
                _add_init(inits, w, cap)

    # Exponential-like skew shapes.
    for r in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0]:
        w1 = np.exp(-r * x) + 1e-6
        w2 = np.exp(-r * (1.0 - x)) + 1e-6
        _add_init(inits, w1, cap)
        _add_init(inits, w2, cap)
        _add_init(inits, w1 + 0.25 * w2, cap)
        _add_init(inits, w2 + 0.25 * w1, cap)
        _add_init(inits, w1 + w2, cap)

    # Random low-frequency shapes.
    for _ in range(5):
        Nf = _next_pow2(n)
        freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        scale = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
        power = 1.0 / (1.0 + (np.arange(len(freq)) * scale * math.sqrt(Nf)) ** 2)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 0.80))
        w = 1.0 + amp * noise
        _add_init(inits, w, cap)

    # Few pure random perturbative seeds.
    for _ in range(3):
        w = 0.15 + 0.85 * rng.random(n)
        _add_init(inits, w, cap)

    # Ensure there is at least one finite candidate.
    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    return inits


def _score_and_sort(inits):
    scores = []
    for i, a in enumerate(inits):
        sc = _exact_score(a)
        scores.append(sc)
    scores = np.asarray(scores, dtype=np.float64)
    order = np.argsort(scores)
    return scores, order


def _polish_sequence(a0, deadline, label=""):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_score = _exact_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    now = time.monotonic()
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(5.0, float(deadline - t0))

    def _advance_best(sc, seq, tag):
        nonlocal best_score, best_a
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _normalize(seq).copy()
            print("[{}] {} improved: {:.10f} | n={} t={:.1f}".format(
                label, tag, best_score, best_a.size, time.monotonic() - t0))

    # Stage 1: smoother LSE.
    stage1_dead = t0 + 0.22 * total
    for beta in [40.0, 250.0, 1200.0]:
        if time.monotonic() >= stage1_dead - 3.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _lbfgs_lse(a, beta, stage1_dead - 2.0)
        _advance_best(sc, a2, "LSE beta={:.0f}".format(beta))
        a = _normalize(a2)

    # Stage 2: exact mirror polish.
    stage2_dead = stage1_dead + 0.30 * total
    for k in [2, 3, 1, 5]:
        if time.monotonic() >= stage2_dead - 3.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _mirror_pass(a, stage2_dead - 2.0, k_active=k, eta_start=2.2)
        _advance_best(sc, a2, "mirror k={}".format(k))
        a = _normalize(a2)

    # Stage 3: sharper LSE.
    stage3_dead = stage2_dead + 0.35 * total
    for beta in [6000.0, 30000.0, 150000.0, 700000.0]:
        if time.monotonic() >= stage3_dead - 3.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _lbfgs_lse(a, beta, stage3_dead - 2.0)
        _advance_best(sc, a2, "LSE beta={:.0f}".format(beta))
        a = _normalize(a2)

    # Stage 4: tight active-set polish.
    rem_dead = max(time.monotonic() + 5.0, deadline - 2.0)
    for k in [1, 2, 3]:
        if time.monotonic() >= rem_dead - 3.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _mirror_pass(a, rem_dead - 2.0, k_active=k, eta_start=0.9)
        _advance_best(sc, a2, "final mirror k={}".format(k))
        a = _normalize(a2)

    return float(best_score), best_a


def _quick_polish(a0, deadline):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    best_score = _exact_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(3.0, float(deadline - t0))

    d1 = t0 + 0.45 * total
    for beta in [2000.0, 20000.0, 150000.0]:
        if time.monotonic() >= d1 - 2.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _lbfgs_lse(a, beta, d1 - 1.0)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _normalize(a2).copy()
        a = _normalize(a2)

    d2 = max(d1 + 1.0, deadline - 1.0)
    for k in [2, 1]:
        if time.monotonic() >= d2 - 2.0:
            break
        sc, a2 = _mirror_pass(a, d2 - 1.0, k_active=k, eta_start=1.0)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = _normalize(a2).copy()
        a = _normalize(a2)

    return float(best_score), best_a


def _perturb(a0, rng, strength=0.20):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))

    if mode == 0:
        noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
        q = a * np.exp(0.12 * strength * noise)
    elif mode == 1:
        root = _root_sequence(n)
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.20))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * root
    elif mode == 2:
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.25))
        q = a * (1.0 + amp * np.sin(math.pi * x))
    elif mode == 3:
        Nf = _next_pow2(n)
        freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        scale = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35))
        power = 1.0 / (1.0 + (np.arange(len(freq)) * scale * math.sqrt(Nf)) ** 2)
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi, size=len(power))
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise -= float(noise.mean())
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25))
        q = a * np.exp(amp * noise)
    elif mode == 4:
        # Small multiplicative log noise, then smooth by one FFT low-pass.
        q = a * np.exp(0.08 * strength * rng.standard_normal(n))
        Nf = _next_pow2(n)
        F = np.fft.rfft(q, Nf)
        freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        F *= np.exp(-(freq * n * 0.25) ** 2 / 2.0)
        q = np.fft.irfft(F, Nf)[:n]
    else:
        # Blend with reversed root.
        root = _root_sequence(n)
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
        q = (1.0 - t) * a + t * root[::-1]

    q = np.clip(np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0, None)
    return _normalize(q)


def _fallback_sequence(n=8192):
    n = int(max(64, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    a = np.power(x, -0.45) * np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-12, 0.25)
    return _normalize(a)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    deadline = t_start + budget_s - 60.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 64:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Choose a large, FFT-friendly length. 16k is usually a good search surface.
    n = 16384
    print("starting search n={} parent_len={}".format(
        n, parent.size if parent is not None else 0))

    inits = _generate_inits(n, rng, parent)
    print("generated {} initial seeds".format(len(inits)))

    scores, order = _score_and_sort(inits)
    finite_order = order[np.isfinite(scores[order])]
    if finite_order.size == 0:
        inits = [_fallback_sequence(n)]
        scores = np.array([_exact_score(inits[0])])
        order = np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
        finite_order = order

    print("best initial seed score={:.10f}".format(float(scores[finite_order[0]])))

    # Select a few diverse-by-score seeds.
    max_starts = min(7, int(finite_order.size))
    start_idx = finite_order[:max_starts]
    starts = [inits[int(i)].copy() for i in start_idx]

    # Free memory for unused seeds.
    try:
        del inits
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_score = float(np.inf)
    best_seq = starts[0].copy()
    for s in starts:
        sc = _exact_score(s)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = s.copy()
    print("initial best={:.10f}".format(best_score))

    # Main optimization: allocate most time to the best-looking starts.
    reserve_main_end = 520.0
    main_end = max(t_start + 700.0, deadline - reserve_main_end)
    total_main = max(300.0, main_end - t_start)

    base_fracs = [0.34, 0.18, 0.13, 0.10, 0.08, 0.07, 0.05]
    base_fracs = base_fracs[:max(1, max_starts)]
    ssum = float(sum(base_fracs))
    fracs = [v / ssum for v in base_fracs]

    cum = 0.0
    for rank, (si, frac) in enumerate(zip(starts, fracs)):
        cum += frac
        sub_dead = t_start + cum * total_main
        sub_dead = min(float(sub_dead), main_end)
        if time.monotonic() >= sub_dead - 40.0:
            continue

        # For later seeds, pull slightly toward current best to encourage convergence.
        if rank == 0:
            sq = si.copy()
        else:
            tr = _resample(best_seq, n)
            t_blend = 0.18 + 0.08 * rank
            sq = (1.0 - t_blend) * _normalize(si) + t_blend * _normalize(tr)
            sq = _normalize(sq)

        sc, seq = _polish_sequence(sq, sub_dead - 8.0, label="start-{}".format(rank + 1))
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = _normalize(seq).copy()
            print("after start-{} best not improved? best={:.10f}".format(rank + 1, best_score))

    best_seq = _normalize(best_seq)
    print("after main best={:.10f} len={}".format(best_score, best_seq.size))

    # Random restarts from best.
    final_dead = deadline - 45.0
    n_restarts = 4
    if budget_s > 800:
        n_restarts = 6
    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 120.0:
        restart_budget_total = max(60.0, final_dead - 60.0 - time.monotonic())
        chunk = restart_budget_total / max(1, n_restarts)
        for r in range(n_restarts):
            chunk_dead = time.monotonic() + chunk
            if time.monotonic() >= final_dead - 80.0:
                break
            p = _perturb(best_seq, rng, strength=0.18)
            sc, seq = _quick_polish(p, min(chunk_dead, final_dead - 30.0))
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = _normalize(seq).copy()
                print("restart {} best={:.10f}".format(r + 1, best_score))

    # Final intensive polishing on the current champion.
    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 25.0:
        d1 = min(final_dead - 35.0, time.monotonic() + max(20.0, 0.35 * (final_dead - time.monotonic())))
        for beta in [30000.0, 250000.0, 1200000.0]:
            if time.monotonic() >= d1 - 3.0:
                break
            sc, seq = _lbfgs_lse(best_seq, beta, d1 - 2.0)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = _normalize(seq).copy()
                print("final LSE beta={:.0f} best={:.10f}".format(beta, best_score))
            best_seq = _normalize(seq)

    if time.monotonic() < final_dead - 8.0:
        for k in [1, 2, 3]:
            if time.monotonic() >= final_dead - 4.0:
                break
            sc, seq = _mirror_pass(best_seq, final_dead - 3.0, k_active=k, eta_start=0.55)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = _normalize(seq).copy()
                print("final mirror k={} best={:.10f}".format(k, best_score))
            best_seq = _normalize(seq)

    # Validate and sanitize output.
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out_arr.sum())
    if S < 0.01:
        out_arr = _fallback_sequence(8192)
        S = float(out_arr.sum())

    if S >= 0.01:
        out_arr = out_arr / S
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
    if len(out) == 0 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    verified = np.inf
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = best_score

    print("FINAL internal={:.10f} verified={:.10f} len={} time={:.1f}s".format(
        best_score,
        verified if np.isfinite(verified) else float("inf"),
        len(out),
        time.monotonic() - t_start
    ))

    # If verified is inf due to an unexpected evaluator issue, return a known good seed.
    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        fb = _fallback_sequence(len(out) if len(out) >= 1024 else 8192)
        fb = _normalize(fb)
        out = [float(v) for v in fb]
        try:
            verified2 = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified2 = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(verified2):
            print("fallback verified score={:.10f}".format(verified2))

    return out
```
